import BaseLayout from '../layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import { Image } from 'astro:assets';
import type { ImageMetadata } from 'astro';
import { siteInfo } from '../data/siteInfo';
import InstagramIcon from '../components/icons/InstagramIcon.astro';
import FacebookIcon from '../components/icons/FacebookIcon.astro';
import CtaBanner from '../components/CtaBanner.astro';

// 1. Load images from src/assets/gallery/
const imageFiles = import.meta.glob<{ default: ImageMetadata }>(
  '../assets/gallery/*.{jpeg,jpg,png,webp}',
  { eager: true }
);

// 2. Parse filenames into readable captions and alt text
const galleryItems = Object.entries(imageFiles).map(([filepath, module]) => {
  const rawFileName = filepath.split('/').pop()?.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '') ?? '';

  const cleanTitle = decodeURIComponent(rawFileName)
    .replace(/[-_]+/g, ' ')
    .trim();

  return {
    image: module.default,
    title: cleanTitle.length > 0 ? cleanTitle : 'Decorating & Restoration Project',
    alt: cleanTitle.length > 0 ? cleanTitle : 'F&B Painting and Decorating project transformation',
  };
});

---

  title="Our Work & Project Gallery | F&B Painting & Decorating"

          </a>

            target="_blank"

          >

            <span>Facebook</span>
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- Gallery Grid -->
  <section class="gallery-section">
    <div class="container">
      <div class="gallery-grid">
        {galleryItems.map((item, index) => (
          <figure class="gallery-card">
            <div class="media-wrapper">
              <Image
                src={item.image}
                alt={item.alt}
                width={650}
                quality="high"
                loading={index < 1 ? "eager" : "lazy"}
                decoding={index < 1 ? "sync" : "async"}
                class="gallery-img"
              />
            </div>
            <figcaption class="card-caption">
              <span>{item.title}</span>
            </figcaption>
          </figure>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <CtaBanner />
</BaseLayout>

=== ./src/pages/about.astro ===

import BaseLayout from '../layouts/BaseLayout.astro';
import { Image } from 'astro:assets';
import faisalVanImg from '../assets/Faisal-Van.jpg';
import CtaBanner from '../components/CtaBanner.astro';

const policyItems = [
  {
    num: "01",
    title: "Job Completion Sign-Off",
    desc: "A completion and sign-off form is reviewed and signed with the client at the end of each project to confirm everything meets our agreed standards."
  },
  {
    num: "02",
    title: "On-Site Walkthrough",
    desc: "A responsible person must be present upon project completion to inspect the finished work, sign off the completion form, and make the final payment."
  },
  {
    num: "03",
    title: "Inspection Continuity",
    desc: "If nobody is available at the property to complete the walkthrough sign-off, the project is paused until an on-site inspection can be arranged."
  },
  {
    num: "04",
    title: "Transparent Extra Work",
    desc: "Any additional work requested outside the original quote is discussed, priced, and formally agreed upon before that work commences."
  },
  {
    num: "05",
    title: "Final Payment at Completion",
    desc: "Full payment is required upon satisfactory completion and sign-off of the work while our team is present on site."
  },
  {
    num: "06",
    title: "Clear Cancellation Notice",
    desc: "If a booking is cancelled with less than 48 hours' notice, a cancellation fee of 10% of the original quotation applies."
  },
  {
    num: "07",
    title: "14-Day Fixed Quotes",
    desc: "All quotations issued by F&B Painting & Decorating Services remain fixed and valid for 14 days from the date of issue."
  },
  {
    num: "08",
    title: "Simple Payment Options",
    desc: "We accept direct bank transfers (BACS) and cash payments upon project sign-off."
  }
];

const coverageTowns = [
  "Wirral", "Liverpool", "Chester", "Ellesmere Port", "Birkenhead",
  "Heswall", "West Kirby", "Upton", "Eastham", "Bromborough",
  "Spital", "Moreton", "Hoylake"
];
---

<script is:inline>
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const btn = document.getElementById('hamburger-btn');
    const drawer = document.getElementById('mobile-drawer');

    if (btn && drawer) {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isOpen = btn.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
        btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!isOpen));
        drawer.classList.toggle('open');
      });
    }
  });
</script>

=== ./README.md ===
# F&B Painting & Decorating Services

A lightning-fast, zero-JS static website built for F&B Painting & Decorating. 

This site is built with [Astro](https://astro.build/) and deployed on Cloudflare Pages. It is heavily optimized for local SEO, mobile performance, and accessibility, achieving a 100/100 Lighthouse score across all metrics.

## 🛠️ How to Update the Site

This project was built to be extremely easy to maintain without touching the core layout code.

### 1. Updating Contact Info & Socials
All global business data is centralized in one file. If Faisal changes his phone number, email, or social links, you only need to update it here:
👉 **`src/data/siteInfo.ts`**

Updating this file will automatically update the Header, Footer, WhatsApp buttons, and SEO meta tags across the entire site.

### 2. Adding to the Gallery
The Portfolio/Gallery page is fully automated. You do **not** need to write any HTML to add new photos.

1. Drop a high-quality image (`.jpg`, `.png`, or `.webp`) into **`src/assets/gallery/`**.
2. **Name the file what you want the caption to be.** Use dashes or underscores for spaces.
   * *Example:* Naming a file `Exterior-timber-sash-window-restoration.jpg` will automatically generate a gallery card with the caption **"Exterior timber sash window restoration"** and apply the correct SEO `alt` text.
3. Astro will automatically resize, compress, and convert the image to WebP during the next build.

## Tech Stack & Performance Notes

* **Framework:** Astro (Static Site Generation)
* **Styling:** Vanilla CSS (Scoped to components)
* **JavaScript:** Zero client-side JS shipped to the browser (except for a tiny inline script for the mobile menu).
* **Hosting:** Cloudflare Pages
* **Assets:** `astro:assets` handles all image optimization and responsive sizing at build time.

## Commands

All commands are run from the root of the project, from a terminal:

| Command                   | Action                                           |
| :------------------------ | :----------------------------------------------- |
| `npm install`             | Installs dependencies                            |
| `npm run dev`             | Starts local dev server at `localhost:4321`      |
| `npm run build`           | Build your production site to `./dist/`          |
| `npm run preview`         | Preview your build locally, before deploying     |


=== ./astro.config.mjs ===
import { defineConfig } from 'astro/config';
import sitemap from '@astrojs/sitemap';

export default defineConfig({
  site: 'https://fbpaintinganddecorating.co.uk',
  trailingSlash: 'never',
  integrations: [sitemap()],
  build: {
    inlineStylesheets: 'always'
  }
});
